18489:         Calendar.FRIDAY to "Friday",
18490:         Calendar.SATURDAY to "Saturday",
18491:         Calendar.SUNDAY to "Sunday"
18492:     )

18494:         "de" -> GermanDays[dayOfWeek] ?: "Montag"
18495:         "ka" -> GeorgianDays[dayOfWeek] ?: "ორშაბათი"
18496:         "zh" -> ChineseDays[dayOfWeek] ?: "周一"
